   	ceṉṉai - 600 006,  maṟṟum pirācacu intiyā, 
tiruvallikkēṇi - 600 005.
yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷai nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalaik kaṭaintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmai kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunilai niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmaikaḷai ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vaittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum kaṟkum tollaiyai mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tillai, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēvaiyumillai. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmaikaḷai ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷaip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉai aṭaiyalām.
- maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nākai - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭaittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulamai mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamaiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam uṭaiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟainta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivait tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittokai, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭaimaiyē eṉṉai ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ tokai curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟaippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷai varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭaiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭai	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷai
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
nuḻaivurai
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
muntai aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum takavārnta perumai peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇaiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yuraikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟai moḻikaḷukkum tantai moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻparappāḷarāyp potunilaik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum perumai peṟṟa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭai malaiyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpatai nilai nāṭṭiya niṟaimalai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
ainteḻuntāl orupāṭai eṉṟu aṟaiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭaiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalaimali pēḻaiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟaimai pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpatai,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunilai tirital ceyyuṭ killai 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇai ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citaintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumaiyum kuṟumaiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷai ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟaimalai aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takainiṉṟa taṇṭamiḻai - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilaikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟaimalai yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talaippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivai
 paintamiḻ kākkum paṭai
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭaiyum kalait tiṟap paṭaippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭaiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟai āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟai āṟṟalai 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalaic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattai
paṉimalai mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amaiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amaintavar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimalai - imayam; nīla āṟu - nail; amaitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷai, nūlaka iyakkat tantai araṅka nātarum, innāḷ caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakaimaippaṭutti vaittamai perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avai,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟaiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭurai 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nākai aṭikaḷai - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattaic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakaiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammaiyār! ivviṇaiyar koṭaiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉai ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷattai vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟaivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvarai payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum nilaiyil, arumait tantaiyār iyaṟkai eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉaiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyait toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmai tamiḻukku illai. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppillai eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟaikaḷaiyum iḷamaiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟaiyaiyum, nākaiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟaiyaiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟaiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟaiyāl vāṭum aṉṉaikkuc cumaiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākaiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamaiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉai māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpataiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyai niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, tokai nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvai, iṟaiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvai mukappil, ematu patiṉaintām āṇṭil muṟaippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōvaiyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷaiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyataiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uraiyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamaip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nākai intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc caiva cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉai maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉaipeyaril maṟuppurai eḻutiṉār. ataṉaikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷaic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉai viraivilē ceṉṉaikku varuvippōm. nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉai vāḻvu amaiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇaivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉai māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇīya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumai kaṇṭu, pērāciriyaraik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uraiyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattaiyum mūtaṟivaiyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanilai ovvāmaiyāl nākaikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉai: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaraic ceṉṉaikku aḻaittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpikai eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉai kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟait talaivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kalaiñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkalai māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullaippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappālai (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci urai eḻutiṉār.
avvurai nūlai vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉait tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟaiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvuraip pāṅkiṉaip piṉpaṟṟip paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷai ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟai urai, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakaikaḷai iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉai viḷaittamai kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakaiya uraikaḷaiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻaintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭattai viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉaik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikkai kuṟaiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟaiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyai muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikaimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. vaiyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikaimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭaivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉai kacaṭukaḷai nīkkiyatu? vaiyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammaic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟaic ceyta arumai eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kārikai āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārait taḻuvutal uṇmai tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapurattai vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkī kāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattaiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattaiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmikaḷ vali yuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkaikaḷaiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷai oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. innilaiyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkaikaḷaiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉaikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉaiyār ciṉṉammaiyār, tuṇaiviyār cavuntiram ammai yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvaraik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpikai, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. innilaiyilēyē 27.08.1911il caivat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭai (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. muṉṉēyē avarai aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvai
nām mīḷpārvai oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akavaiyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kaivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullaippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappālai ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvurai vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉai; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉai; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉai - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikaiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭamai eṉpatu, akattē amaintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷai viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyām. av veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravillai! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka nilaiyam, potunilaik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amaippu uruvākum nilaiyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avai iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa nilaiyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭait tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭait tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭait tūṇṭal valimai mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyai veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakaittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takaimai ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉai uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilaināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amaintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmai,
  tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟai yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantaip pēṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉaippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟokai, arumaṇik kuvaiyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷaik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, caivacittānta nūṟ patippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumaimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumait tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 varai mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavai. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyavaiyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭaimai ākkap paṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vaitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉamai, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷai mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. annilaiyil,
  ūḻaiyum uppakkam kāṇpar ulaiviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyai vāymai moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷaiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amaintu tāṅkal, iyaṟkai iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟai muṉvaittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icaik kalaikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākarattai veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉai vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉaip pakutikaḷaiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, uraivēntar auvai cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷai vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantaiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷaiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷaiyum muḻumai muḻumaiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - tokai - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷaiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti varai veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷai ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amaikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumai iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇai yaiyum makkaḷaiyum uṟavaiyum aṉpaiyum naṇpaiyum oruṅkē iṇaittu, aṟiñar peru makkaḷ palar aravaṇaippaiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
maṟaimalaiyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumai! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalaic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭait tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putaiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉaiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭai! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakaimai! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟaiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymai viḷakkamām!
puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar nilaiyam,						                    iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							                irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturai - 6
625006.

malaikka vaikkum maṟaimalaiyam!
patippurai
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉai vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmai uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷatai varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇarai aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭaiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattait tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭai eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭai niṉaivu kūrum vakaiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyai eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭaippukaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimaiyūṭṭiṉar.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻai 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭaippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavai, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavai, peyar terintum pārvaikkuk kiṭṭātavai - eṉap palavakaiyāyk kiṭaittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭaippukaḷ! avaṟṟai `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum talaippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭaivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavalaiyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt ticai nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭaipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭaiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputaiyal palar kaikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷārait tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyattai etirppatilum camaṟkirutattai vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyait tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vaittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavaittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻaippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimaiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukaiyaiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kalaiñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitaikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvai avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta virutai civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamaippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malaimalaiyaṭikaḷ).
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyaraik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟaimiku āṅkilam maṟaivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulamai mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻaikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumaiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
  vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
  maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷ maṟaiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉaip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉai maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkaiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻaippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamillai.
ceṉṉaiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷait tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talaimuṟaikkum varuṅkālat talaimuṟaikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉai (cātaṉai)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumaiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avai kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpatai aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalattai otukki vaittu, tamiḻ nalattaik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟaivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalaip piṉṉiṇaippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻaikkum uṇarvai iḷamaiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmai eḻa vaittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avarai eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamaiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟaivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavai! avaṟṟil orupakutiyai yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kaikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vai. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvattai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉaivar kaikaḷaiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumai - caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟai aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟai aṉaivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlaic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭaikkum paṇiyaic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumaiyaik karutātu uḻaikkum maṉavurattai muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷai iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭaiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
  vallamai cērkkum valimaiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ naintiṭil etunammaik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻaik kākkum uṇarvaip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kaikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷai nāṭṭuṭaimaiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.
`carivum iḻappum namakku vantālum tamiḻukku varakkūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi uḻaikkum iyakkaṅkaḷum amaippukaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaṟṟāmal vaḷarntapaṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa.

tamiḻ malarccikkum taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum aṭittaḷam amaitta ittaku amaippukaḷukku mūla vicaiyākat tikaḻum perumaiyaip peṟuvōr maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārum pāvāṇarumē!
tamiḻ vaḷattiṟkum tamiḻar nalattiṟkum civaṉiya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷappariyavai. avai toṭarpāka avar āyntaḷitta aṟivu muttukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavai!
aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉaittup paṭaippukaḷum poruḷvaḻip pirikkappaṭṭu, kālavaricaiyil 34 tokuppukaḷākat tokukkap paṭṭu `maṟaimalaiyam eṉa uṅkaḷ kaikaḷil ippōtu tavaḻkiṉṟatu.

cākuntala nāṭaka ārāycci

1960il caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam patippitta
nūliṉai mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
innūlaip paṟṟiya kuṟippurai...
kāḷitācariṉ kālam, tiṟaṉ ākiyavaṟṟai innūlil aṭikaḷār ārāyntuḷḷār. nāṭaka iyalpu, nāṭaka varalāṟu, nāṭaka amaippu, nāṭakak katai yamaippu, nāṭaka māntar iyaṟkai. katai nikaḻum iṭamum kālamum, nāṭaka amaippiṟ kāṇappaṭum cila kuṟaipāṭukaḷ, kāḷitācar kālappaḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ eṉap pala kūṟukaḷākac cākuntala nāṭaka ārāycci amaintuḷḷatu.
innūliṉ nāṭakak kalai taṉittum oppīṭṭu nilaiyilum ārāyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
muṉṉai nāḷ nallicaip pulavarkaḷ, ulakiyaṟkai, makkaḷ iyaṟkaikaḷiṉ uṇmai vaṉappukaḷai uṇarntu uṇarttum meyyaṟivu mēṉmai mikkavarkaḷ. ittaku iyalpiṉāl iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ciṟanta nallicaip pulavarāka viḷaṅkukiṟār. kāḷitācariṉ mutirnta pulamaiyum, nāṭaka nūl nuṭpaṅkaḷ naṉkuṇarnta uṇarvum kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa vākum. ivvāṟu aṭikaḷāriṉ cākuntala nāṭaka ārāycci aṟivu nalam perukkum ariya ārāycciyākum. aḻakiya iṉiya eḷiya tamiḻ naṭaiyaiyum innūl koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
- ṭākṭar nā. ceyappirakācu
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ ilakkiyappaṭaippukaḷ (pak. 19)


PREFACE
“A play attains artistic perfection just in proportion as it approaches that unity of lyrical effect, as if a sony or ballad were still lying at the root of it, all the various expression of the conflict of character and circumstance calling at last into the compass of a single melody or musical theme.” - Walter Pater.
What Pater, the profound critic and the master of an inimitable English prose style, has said in the above extract taken from his essay on ‘Shakespeare’s English Kings’ applies closely to the Sakuntala the famous drama of the Sanscrit poet Kalidasa. The theme of the drama is that which, with all the lyrical charm, treats of the deep love that had sprung between Sakuntala who lived in the hermitage of her foster-father removed far away from the busy haunts of men and the king Dushyanta who accidentally visited her there while he was on a hunting campaign in the forests at the foot of the Himalayas. The plot of the drama is not variegated, as in the comedies of Shakespeare by any artistically inwoven under plots, but is extremely simple and by means of the passionate love-songs that appear all through and by its easily comprehensible unity produces in effect markedly lyrical. Nevertheless, the interest of the plot rises at the point where the poet complicates the situation by skilfully introducing into it the curse of the sage Durvasa and making it appear for a time as if the unity had been broken by that unforeseen circumstance and by the conflict of character exhibited between an innocent young woman absorbed in her recent love experience and the easily irritated sage who had suddenly appeared before her. Still the lyrical unity runs clearly through the succeeding events by the interest of the plot being attached anew to the loss and recovery of the ring which the king put on the finger of Sakuntala when he returned to his capital. From this it must be evident how artistically the poet Kalidasa has, for the purpose of the play, perfected this simple story taken from the Mahabharata, the great store-house of the Indian legends, by introducing into it the curse    of Durvasa which is not found in its original source but which has been skilfully created by the poet himself. Though the creation of this incident looks admirable, so far as it seves as an indispensable hinge on which the plot turns, I might be permitted to question whether it is not inconsistent with the stainless character of a saintly person. I have discussed this point in the following critical study of the Sakuntala and have shown how Shakespeare stands superior to Kalidasa in this respect.
But in the art of delineating the main and the sub-ordinate characters that appear in this drama kalidasa is not in any way inferior to Shakespeare but to a remarkable degree is his equal. With what great acuteness Kalidasa has penetrated to the inward nature of each character, in what brief, pointed expressions of thought and feeling he has so well brought it out, and in what strong relief he sets one in contrast with or foil for the other, can be grasped only by those who make an attentive and discerning study of the speeches of each personage. To the best of my understanding I have, in the following critique, shown at  length these psychological differences which the poet has so carefully observed and noted in his characters.
Years ago I happened to hear in English lecture delivered by an able brahmin scholar on this drama of Kalidasa. After dwelling on some of the excellences of the play, when he came to speak of the character - study of the poet, he, instead of going right through the speeches manners and actions of each dramatic  personage, and trying to exhibit whether or not there were special features in each, summarily dismissed it by saying that Kalidasa was sadly wanting in his delineation of character and is therefore no peer of Shakespeare the prince of the dramatists. That sweeping remark of the learned lecturer kindled in me a burning desire to make a comparative study of the dramatic art of the two poets, and from that time onward I have been reading critically not only the plays of Kalidasa and Shakespeare but also the commentaries on their plays. Unfortunately in the whole range of either Sanscrit or English Literature I could not meet even a single commentary on Kalidasa’s dramas that is worth mentioning along side of the very interesting and illuminating Shakespearian commentaries produced by Ulrici, Gervinus, Schlegel, Swinburne, Dowden, Brooke, Bradley, Symons, Hudson, Moulton, Corson, Brandes and others. In this life no one can experience a pleasure comparable in its height, amount and intensity to the intellectual and aesthetic pleasure which a study of these celebrated commentaries afford. The pleasure given by these is equal only to that yielded by the plays themselves, nay, I may venture to say it is even a degree higher than that. In this class of brilliant Shakespearian commentaries must be included also J.A. Symond’s critical estimates both of the British dramatists and of the old Greek tragedians Aeschylus, Sophocles and Euripides, since these mark an epoch of higher dramatic criticism.
The great literary pleasure experienced in the study of these Shakespearian commentaries, impelled me to attempt after their model a deep and discriminating study of the dramatic art of Kalidasa, a Tamil translation of whose mature production the Sakuntala I produced in the year 1906 and a second edition of which is now issued with notes and the following critique which embodies the results of my study. This and my similar critical commentaries on the ancient Tamil Idylls the Mullaippattu, and the Pattinappalai, I believe, may serve to remove to a certain extent the reproach that the modern Tamil literature is sadly barren of higher critical prose writings. If the reader finds this my claim justifiable, I shall consider my labour as sufficiently recompensed.
In the following critique, besides showing the merits of Kalidasa’s dramatic art, I have also fixed his date in the first half of the fifth century A.D. basing it on the indisputable epigraphical evidence and co-ordianting this evidence by that afforded by the work itself.
Finally I am glad to say that, in tracing the origin and growth of the drama in India, I quite accidentally lighted on the discovery that this art took its rise not from among the alien Aryas but from the indigenous Tamils themselves. I believe I have in the following critique sufficiently explained all the facts that led me to this most important discovery.
Pallavaram,	  VEDACHALAM
6-1-1934

1. nāṭaka iyalpu
vaṭamoḻi nallicaippulavarāṉa kāḷitāca riyaṟṟiya cākuntala nāṭaka amaippiṉ tiṟaṉum nuṭpamum vaṉappum payaṉum ārāyntu teḷivataṟkumuṉ, nāṭakattiṉ iyalpum aḵtu ipparatanāṭṭiṉ kaṭṭōṉṟiya varalāṟun terintu koḷḷal iṉṟiyamaiyātatākaliṉ, mutalil nāṭakattiṉ iyalpu iṉṉateṉpataṉai oru ciṟitu viḷakkuvām. āṟaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷum avarallāta ēṉai iyaṅkum uyirkaḷuñ ceyyum muyaṟcikaḷ ellām aṟivu muyaṟciyun toḻiṉ muyaṟciyum eṉṉum iruperum pirivil vantu aṭaṅkiniṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu ellāvuyirkaḷ māṭṭuṅ kāṇappaṭum ivviruvakai muyaṟci kaḷum potuvākat tuṉpa nīkkattaiyum iṉpa ākkattaiyumē avāvi niṟpaṉavākum. ivvuyirkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum potuvāka uḷḷa tuṉpaṅkaḷ paciyum kāmamumākavē  kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. pacittuṉpattai uṇaviṉālum, kāmattuṉpattai āṇum peṇṇumāy oruṅku maruvutalāṉum pōkki, avvāṟṟāl ellā uyirkaḷum iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntu varutalaik kaṇkūṭāka nām kaṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉam allamō? iṅṅaṉamākat tamakku iyaṟkaiyē eḻum iruvakait tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum nīkki avvāṟṟāl iruvēṟu iṉpaṅkaḷai nukarum iyaṅkum uyirkaḷ avviruvakaiyiṉpa nukarcci yaḷavil amaiti peṟāmal, vēṟumōr iṉpattai nukartalil viḻaivu mikuntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. aḵtiyātōveṉil, avai tān tām virumpu māṟellām viḷaiyāṭi nukarum iṉpamēyām. pārmiṉkaḷ! paciyuṅ kāmanukarcciyun tīrntapiṟaku pūṉai nāy mutaliya uyirkaḷum evvaḷavu kiḷarcciyuṭaṉ viḷaiyāṭi iṉpuṟu kiṉṟaṉa! pūṉaiyāṉatu maraṅkaḷaip piṟāṇṭiyum, patuṅkit tuḷḷiyum viḷaiyāṭutalai evarum pārttirukkalām. tōḻamaikoṇṭa iraṇṭu nāykaḷ poyyāka oṉṟai yoṉṟu kauviyum. oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṉkālkaḷāṟ piṇaintum, oṉṟaiviṭ ṭoṉṟu appuṟaṅ kutittōṭi mīṇṭu vantu kauviyum, oṉṟai yoṉṟu nōkkiyapaṭiyāyp patuṅkiyum, oṉṟu oṉṟaṉ mēṟ pāyntum iṅṅaṉamellām viḷaiyāṭṭayarntu makiḻtalai evarum pārttirukkalām. iṉṉum veḷi nilaṅkaḷilum malaic cāraliṉ kaṇ uḷḷa paḷḷattākkukaḷilum mēyum āṭumāṭukaḷ pul mēyntapiṉ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭiyum, oṉṟaṉmēloṉṟēṟiyum, oṉṟu turatta maṟṟoṉṟu vālaik kiḷappikkoṇṭu ōṭiyum, tuḷḷiyum viḷaiyāṭik kaḷittalai aṅkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷ  pārttirukkalām.
iṅṅaṉamē makkaḷāyp piṟanta maṉṉuyirkaḷum pala vakaiyāṉa viḷaiyāṭalkaḷāl tam oḻivu nēraṅkaḷaik kaḷippuṭaṉ kaḻittu varukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷallāta maṟṟai yuyirkaḷ pakuttaṟivu illātaṉavākaliṉ, avai tammuṭaiya viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷai ōr oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil viḷaiyāṭavum, avai tammaip piṟavuyirkaḷukku oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil viḷaiyāṭik kāṭṭavum  vallaṉa alla. maṟṟu, makkaḷō tamakkuḷḷa pakuttaṟivu vaṉmaiyāl tām viḷaiyāṭum vakaikaḷai ōroḻuṅkupaṭa amaittukkoḷvatuṭaṉ, tammō ṭottārkkum avai tammai ōr oḻuṅkuṟak kāṭṭi avarkkup perumakiḻcciyiṉai viḷaivittum varukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu makkaḷ ōr oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil viḷaiyāṭum viḷaiyāṭalkaḷ attaṉaiyum mutalil evvāṟu tōṉṟalāyiṉa veṉiṉ; mutirnta paruvattiṉarāyirukkum makkaḷ tuvakkattil enta nilaiyiṉarāyiruntu evvāṟu viḷaiyāṭiṉareṉpatai ārāyavē avaitōṉṟiya vuṇmai teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
pārmiṉkaḷ! nām viḷaiyāṭum paruvattuc ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷāy irunta ñāṉṟu eṅṅaṉam viḷaiyāṭiṉōm? cōṟu camaikkum viḷaiyāṭṭum, `peṇṇu māppiḷai vaittu maṇañ ceyvikkum viḷaiyāṭṭum allavō viḷaiyāṭiṉam? ēṉaic ciṟṟuyirkaḷaippōlavē, makkaḷum paci kāmam uṭaiyar; eṉṟālum, pacittapōtu oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil uṇavu camaittu uṇṇavuṅ, kāmavēṭkai yeḻuntapōtu oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil āṇum peṇṇumāka maṇantu kāmaviṉpam nukaravum makkaḷ oḻuṅkāṉa aṟivumuyaṟci vāyntavarāyiruttalpōla, vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ vāyntavākāmai teḷiyakkiṭanta toṉṟām, makkaḷ ceyyum muṟaiyāṉa aṟivumuyaṟcikaḷ ellām ivvāṟu camaiyaṟṟoḻilum maṇaviṉaiyum eṉṉum iraṇṭil vantaṭaṅki oḻuṅkāka naṭaipeṟak kāṇṭaliṉ, avar tañciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷun tāmuñ cōṟu camaittum peṇṇu māppiḷai vaittu maṇampurivittum viḷaiyāṭṭu ayarā niṟkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, piṟparuvattil makkaḷ ayarum palvēṟu viḷaiyāṭalkaḷuk kellām muṟpaṭṭu mutalāyniṟpaṉa ciṟār ayarum iv viruvakai viḷaiyāṭalkaḷēyā meṉpatu teṟṟeṉa uṇarntu koḷḷappaṭu meṉka.
iṉit, tam peṟṟā ruṟṟār ceyyuñ ceyalkaḷaik kaṇṭu avaṟṟaippōl tāmuñ ceytu viḷaiyāṭṭu ayarvatiṟ ciṟumakārkku uṇṭām makiḻcciyiṉ iyalpiṉai āḻntu ārāyuṅkāl, ōr oḻuṅkāṉa amaippaiyum oru kiḷarcciyāṉa ceyalaiyuṅ kaṇṭu avaipōṟṟāmuñ ceytalilum, allatu avaipōṟ ceytu kāṭṭappaṭuṅ kāṭci yilumāka makkaḷ peritum iṉpuṟuntaṉmaiyareṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākāniṟkiṉṟatu. ciṟumakār tam peṟṟōr ceyyuñ camaiyaṟ ṟoḻilaip pārttut tāmum maṇaṟcōṟu camaittu iṉpuṟutal pōlavē āṇṭu mutirntavarkaḷun tammāṟ pārāṭṭappaṭuvār ceyyum nalviṉai tīviṉaikaḷaip pārttu avarpōṟ ṟāmuñ ceytu iṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. kalviyaṟiviluñ col vaṉmaiyiluñ ciṟantār oruvar ōravaikkaḷattēṟik kaṭavuḷiṉ aḷaviṟanta āṟṟalaiyum avar eṇṇiṟanta ulakaṅkaḷaiyellām vāṉveḷiyiṟ pantukaḷpōl vaittuc cuḻaṟṟutalaiyum av vulakaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum uḷḷa eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷukkum eṇṇiṟanta viyattakum uṭampukaḷaik koṭuttu avar avai tamakku aṟivuviḷaṅkac ceytuvarum aruṭṭiṟaṅkaḷaiyum, nām  nam kaṇṇetirē kāṇpatupōl eṭutturaittu nam aṟivaiyum aṉpaiyuṅ kavarntu ceṉṟakkāl, nām ataṉāl avar vayamāki avaraippōṟ pēcavum avaraip pōṟ kaikāl acaikkavum viḻaikiṉṟaṉam allamō? avviḻaivu mītūrap peṟṟakkāl avarpōl avaikaḷaic ceyyak kaṟṟu nām makiḻtalum uṇṭaṉṟō? iṅṅaṉam nalviṉai ceyvāraik kaṇṭu nāmum avar pōṟ ceyyavuñ ceytu makiḻavum muṉaintu niṟṟal pōlavē, tīviṉai ceyvāraik kaṇṭum avarpōṟ ceyyavuñ ceytu makiḻavum muṉaintu  niṟpārum ulakil mikap palarā yirukkiṉṟaṉar. kaṭavuḷ illaiyeṉṟum, ellām iyaṟkaiyē yeṉṟum, ūṉ uṇṭal, kaṭkuṭittal, varai tuṟaiyiṉṟi maruval, ēmāntavar poruḷaik kavartal, ēmāṟātavaraiyum ēmāṟṟutal, aṟivil ēṟṟat tāḻviṉmai yiṉ oruvar ēṉaiyoruvarkku aṭaṅkā toḻukutal mutaliya viṉaikaḷaiyellām avaravar tattam vaṉmai meṉmaikaṭ kēṟpac ceytal kuṟṟa mākāteṉṟum āravārat tōṭu uraiyāṭuvār tiṟattaik kaṇṭu avarai viyappārum, avar pōṟceyya virumpu vārum, ceytoḻukuvārum varavara mikutal kāṇṭumaṉṟō? iṉṉum oru malaic cāralai yum ataṉ kīḻuḷḷatōr ērik karaiyil māṉmantaikaḷ nīrarunti niṟṟalaiyuṅ kaṇṭu makiḻum makiḻcciyaiviṭa, ak kāṭciyiṉai eḷḷaḷavum picakāmal varaintu vaittirukkum ōr ōviyattiṉaik kaṇṭu ataṉai viyantu makiḻum makiḻcci nampāl mikap peritā yirukkiṉṟa taṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamē, nām ōr aracaṉaiyum, avaṉ araci yaiyum, avaṉṟaṉ amaiccar paṭaittalaivar paṭaiyāṭ kaḷaiyum, avaṉai etirkkum avaṉṟaṉ māṟṟaracar avaṉuk kuriyārkaḷ mutalāyiṉāraiyuṅ kāṇa nērum pōtum, avar uraiyāṭuvaṉavaṟṟaik kēṭka nērum pōtum, avar ceyyuñ ceyalkaḷaip pārkka nērum pōtum accattoṭu viraviya oruvakai makiḻcciyiṉai aṭaikiṉṟaṉarēṉum, am makiḻcci, avarkaḷaippōṟ kōlampūṇṭu avarkaḷaip pōl uraiyāṭi avarkaḷaip pōl naṭamāṭikkāṭṭum nāṭakakkāraraik kaṇṭu nām aṭaiyum makiḻccikku īṭāka māṭṭātu. ākavē, uṇmaiyāka uḷḷa oru tōṟṟat tiṉaiyum, uṇmaiyāka nikaḻum oru nikaḻcciyiṉaiyuṅ kaṇṭu eytum iṉpattiṉum avaṟṟaip pōlvaṉavān tōṟṟattaiyum nikaḻcciyaiyuṅ kaṇṭu eytum iṉpamē makkaḷuḷḷattaip perituṅ kavarum peṟṟiyatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉa ōrntukoḷka.
iṉṉum, uṇmaiyāṉatoru tōṟṟattaiyum nikaḻcci yaiyuṅ kaṇṭu kaḷikkuṅkālum, nām avaṟṟaik kāṇum avvaḷavil amaitipeṟāmal, avaṟṟōṭotta aḻakiya tōṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum nikaḻccikaḷaiyum annērattil uṭaṉ niṉaintō niṉaintu colliyō mēlum mikak kaḷitta lilē mika muṉaintu niṟkiṉṟaṉam allamō? tiruttamāṉa amaippum poṉniṟamum vāyntu tikaḻum oru maṅkaiyaik kāṇpavar, avaḷait tām kaṇṭu makiḻuṅkāl ā! ivaḷ oru poṟpāvaiyai yottup polikiṉṟāḷ! eṉṟu avaḷukku oru poṟpāvaiyai oppāka eṭuttuc colli viyantu makiḻtalaik kāṇṭumallamō? piṉṉēyuḷḷa cākuntala nāṭakattiluñ cakuntalaiyiṉ vaṭivaḻakaik kaṇṭa tuṣiyanta maṉṉaṉ,
mōvā malarō nakaṅkaḷai yāta muḻumuṟiyō!
āvā karuvi turuvātu peṟṟa arumaṇiyō!
nāvāṟ cuvaiyāp putunaṟa vō! ceyta naṟṟavaṅkaḷ
tāvā toruṅku tiraṇṭuvan tālaṉṉa taiyalarē!
eṉṟu avaḷatu putteḻil nalattiṉ iyalpukku otta pala arumperum putup poruḷkaḷai uvamaiyāka eṭuttuc collip peritu makiḻtalkāṇka. ivvāṟē, maṉak kavarcciyiṉai viḷaikkum enta uyarnta tōṟṟattai enta aḻakiya poruḷaik kaṇṭālum, avaṟṟōṭotta piṟaporuṭṭōṟṟattaiyum aḻakaiyum oruṅku niṉaintu makiḻtalē makkaḷuḷḷattiṉ iyaṟkai nikaḻcciyākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. innikaḻcciyiṉ mutaṟṟōṟṟamē ciṟāriṭattu maṇaṟcōṟṟu viḷaiyāṭṭākavum marappāvai maṇamākavum mukiḻkkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, ulakanaṭaiyil mutaṉmaiyuṟṟut tōṉṟum iruvakai nikaḻccikaḷai iṅṅaṉam oppumai vakaiyāl uḷḷaṭakkiya ivviruvakaic ciṟār viḷaiyāṭṭuk kaḷiliruntē, immalar talaiyulakiṟ palatalaimuṟaiyāyp parantu virinta nikaḻccikaḷai yellāṅ kaṇṇāṭipōṟ ṟaṉṉakattē potintuvaittu viḷaṅkak kāṭṭum nāṭakam ākiya māntar viḷaiyāṭṭut tōṉṟalāyiṟṟeṉṟuṇartal vēṇṭum, nāṭakam eṉṉuñcol viḷaiyāṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷai yuṭaiyateṉpataṟku māṇikka vācakap perumāṉ aruḷicceyta,
  pukavē takēṉ uṉak kaṉparuḷ yāṉeṉpol lāmaṇiyē!
  takavē eṉaiyuṉak kāṭkoṇṭa taṉmaiyep puṉmaiyarai
  mikavē yuyarttiviṇ ṇōraip paṇittiaṇ ṇāvamutē!
  nakavē takumem pirāṉeṉṉai nīceyta nāṭakamē! 
(tiruccatakam, 10)
eṉṉun tiruvācakac ceyyuḷē cāṉṟām. iṉṉum, potu makkaḷ ellārum nāṭakattai āṭṭam eṉṟu vaḻaṅki varutalum uṟṟunōkkaṟpāṟṟu. iṉṉum itu kuti eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṟṟēṉṟiya kūttu eṉṉuñ collāṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, viḷaiyāṭṭu, nāṭakam, āṭṭam, kūttu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷellām mutalil orē poruḷai yuṇartuvaṉavāyp, piṉṉar nāṭakamuṟaikaḷ palkap palkac, ciṟitu ciṟitu poruḷ vēṟṟumai yuṭaiyavāyiṉa veṉka.
iṉi nāṭakam āṭumvakai tuvakkattiliruntē ev vevvāṟu māṟutaleyti varalāyiṟṟeṉpatu ārāyaṟ pāṟṟu. oruvaṉaiyum oruttiyaiyum vaittu maṇam puritalaik kāṇuñ ciṟārkaḷ tāmum aṅṅaṉam maṇañ ceyvittu viḷaiyāṭat toṭaṅkukaiyil, maṇamakaṉukku aṭai yāḷamāka āṇvaṭivāyc camaitta oru marappāvai yiṉaiyum maṇamakaḷukku aṭaiyāḷamākap peṇvaṭivāyc camaitta oru marappāvaiyiṉaiyum oruṅkuvaittu, avai tamakkut tām aṟintavāṟu āṭaiyaṇikalaṉkaḷ alaṅkal cātti oppaṉai ceytu viḷaiyāṭal kaṇṭāmaṉṟō? atupōlavē, āṇṭil mutirnta iḷaiñarum piṟarun tām mutaṉmutaṟ ṟuvaṅkiya nāṭakattaiyum marappāvaikaḷ koṇṭē naṭattik kāṭṭiṉar. itu pāvaik kūttu eṉṟum pommalāṭṭam eṉṟum iṉṟu kāṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. yām ciṟu piḷḷaiyā yirunta kālattil ariccantiranāṭakam muṟṟum aḻakiya pāvaik kūttil vaittut tiṟampaṭa naṭattappaṭṭa taṉaip pārttu makiḻntirukkiṉṟēm. 
iṅṅaṉam marappāvaiyāṟ camaitta pāvaikaḷaik koṇṭu nāṭakam naṭattappaṭutal pōlavē, tōlāṟ camaitta pāvaikaḷaik koṇṭum nāṭakam naṭattappaṭutal paṇṭaik kālantoṭṭē yuḷateṉpatu cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟakkātaikku aṭiyārkku nallār uraitta vuraiyāl naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. āṉālum, iv viruvakaik kūttil marappāvaik kūttē ēṉait tōṟpāvaik kūttukkum muṟpaṭṭa teṉpatu, tōṟpāvaik kūttun tollai marappāvai yiyakkamum eṉṉuñ civañāṉa cittic ceyyuḷiṟ paḻamaipporuṭarun tollai eṉṉuñ col marappāvaik kūttukku aṭaimoḻiyāy niṟṟalāl teḷiyappaṭum. eṉavē, marappāvai tōṟpāvaikaḷālēyē mutaṉmutal nāṭakam naṭattik kāṭṭappaṭṭateṉpatu uṇarntukoḷka. vaṭamoḻip paḻanūlākiya māpāratattiṉ kaṇṇum  (3,30,23,5,39,1) pāvaikkūttu moḻiyap paṭṭiruttalāṉum, āciriyar kuṇāṭṭiyarālē iyaṟṟappaṭṭa pirukatkataiyil acurarkkut taccaṉāṉa mayaṉ eṉpavaṉiṉ putalvi taṉ ṟōḻikkup pāvaikkūttuk kāṭṭi avaḷai makiḻvippavaḷ eṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭiruttalāṉum itu vaṭanāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum paṇṭaināḷiṟ paraviyiruntamai naṉkuṇarappaṭu meṉka.
iṉi, uyiraṟṟa pāvaikkūttil vaittu māntariṉ ulakiyaloḻukkattai naṭattik kāṭṭuvatu pēruḻaip piṉaiyum peruvaruttattiṉaiyun taruvatallāmalum, uyiruḷḷa makkaḷiṉ kuṇaṅkuṟikaḷai uṇmaiyil uḷḷapaṭiyē pulappaṭuttutaṟkum ākāmaiyiṉ avviṭarppāṭu kaṇṭu, piṉṉar iḷaiñarkaḷaiyē nāṭakamāṭap paḻakki avarkaḷ palavakaik kōlampūṇṭu oru kataiyiṉai naṭattik kāṭṭumāṟu aṟiñar vakuttaṉar. ivvāṟu makkaḷē nāṭakam āṭak kaṟṟukkoṇṭapiṉ, itu mikuntuvarap pāvaikkūttu nāḷaṭaivil aruki varalāyiṟṟu, eṉṟālum, pāvaikkūttu muṟṟumē maṟaintu pōkāmal, iṭaiyiṭaiyē iṉṟukāṟum naṭaipeṟṟu varutalai orōviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. 
aṟṟēl aḵtāka kūttiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum vēṟupāṭu eṉṉaiyeṉiṟ kūtteṉpatu oru kataiyiṉ oru pakutiyai oruvarēṉum aṉṟi orucilarēṉum āṭik kāṭṭuvatuṅ, kataiyē taḻuvātu oru ceyyuṭ poruḷaik kaṇ kai kāl mukam mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷāl āṭik kāṭṭuvatum, viḻākkālaṅkaḷil oruvarēṉum orucilarēṉum pāṭṭuṅ koṭṭuṅkoṇṭu makiḻccimikku āṭuvatum eṉap palatiṟappaṭum. itaṉ virivellāñ cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟu kātaikku aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyavuraiyiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka. maṟṟu, nāṭakam eṉpatō muḻuvatūuṅ katai taḻuvivarum kūttākum, iḵtu aṭiyārkku nallāruraiyāṉum naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkum. eṉṟālum, nāṭakam eṉṉuñcol orōvoru kāṟ katai taḻuvāta kūttiṉmēṟṟāyum varutal kaḻutoṭu kāṭṭiṭai nāṭakam āṭi eṉṉun tiruvācakattāl (tiruccatakam, 7) viḷaṅkāniṟkiṉṟatu.
2. nāṭaka varalāṟu
iṉi, mutalmutal nāṭakamāṉatu civapirāṉālum umaip pirāṭṭiyārālum ākkappaṭṭateṉṟum, civapirāṉāl vakukkappaṭṭatu. tāṇṭavam eṉṉum peyarttāy āṇ makkaḷ mummuramāy āṭuṅ kūttāmeṉṟum, umaip pirāṭṭi yārāl vakukkappaṭṭatu. lāyam eṉṉum peyarttāy nilattiṉiṉṟum mēleḻāta aṭikaḷiṉ acaivoṭu muṉṉum piṉṉumāka āṭicceṉṟu kai kaṇ puruvam itaḻ mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷāṟ kātal nikaḻcciyiṉaip pulappaṭutti icaiyuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉitu naṭaipeṟuṅ kūttāmeṉṟun tacarūpakaṅ kūṟāniṟkiṉṟatu (1,10) iṅṅaṉamē muttirārāṭcacamum mālatimātavamum nuvalutal kāṇka. kāḷitācarun tāmiyaṟṟiya maṟṟoru nāṭakamākiya māḷavikākki nimittirattiṟ kaṇatācar eṉṉum nāṭaka āciriyar vāyilākac civapirāṉum ammai yumē nāṭakattai mutaṉ mutal ākkiṉōrāva reṉpataṉait teḷivuṟuttal karuttiṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. mēluṅ, ki.mu.700ām āṇṭil aḵtāvatu iṟṟaikku 2600 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ iruntavarāka muṭivu ceyyap paṭṭirukkiṉṟa pāṇiṉi muṉivar1 tamatu aṭṭāttiyāyi yiṟ kuṟippiṭṭirukkum naṭa cūttiraṅkaḷ eṉṉum nūl iññāṉṟu nāṭṭiya cātiram eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu, eṉavē, innūl iṟṟaikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē iyaṟṟappaṭṭa mikap paḻaiya nūlātal tāṉē pōtarum. ittuṇaip paḻaiyatākiya nāṭṭiya cātirattiṉ kaṇ tiripuratakaṉam eṉṉum nāṭakak kāppiyaṅ kuṟippiṭṭiruttalāṟ, paṇṭaik kālattirunta muṉṉōrkaḷ mutaṉ mutal āṭakkaṇṭu kaḷittatu civapirāṉ muppuraṅkaḷai eritta katai taḻuviya nāṭakamēyātal teṟṟeṉap pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? akkatai taḻuviya nāṭakam attuṇaip paḻaiyatāy iruttal paṟṟiyaṉṟē piṟkālat tirunta vatcarājaṉ (ki.pi.1163) eṉṉum nāṭakap pulavaṉum, appeyarpūṇṭa tiripuratākam eṉṉum nāṭakak kāppiyat tiṉai iyaṟṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉeṉpatu, eṉṟivvāṟu tamiḻarkaḷ vaṇaṅkiya tamiḻtteyvamākiya civapirāṉum ammaiyumē nāṭakattai mutaṉmutal ākkiṉavar kaḷeṉṉum varalāṟu paṇṭutoṭṭu varutalkoṇṭuñ, civapirāṉ muppuram eritta nikaḻcciyē mutaṉmutal nāṭakamākac ceytu naṭittuk kāṭṭap paṭṭateṉṟu vaṭamoḻip paḻanūlākiya nāṭṭiya cāttiraṅ kūṟutal koṇṭum paṇṭaikkālattil nāṭakanūl uṇṭāyatu tamiḻmakkaḷuḷḷē tām eṉṟu aṟitalvēṇṭum. tamiḻil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa paḻaiya nāṭaka nūlkaḷ iññāṉṟu kāṇakkiṭaiyāmai paṟṟiyum, ikkālattu vaḻaṅkum nāṭakak kāppiyaṅka ḷellām āriyam pirākirutam arttamākati marāṭṭi mutalāṉa vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷil eḻutap paṭṭiruttal paṟṟiyum nāṭaka nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻikkē yuriyavai pōlumeṉṟu malaiyaṟka. ēṉeṉiṉ, vaṭakkēvantu kuṭiyēṟiya āriyarkaḷ naṭaṉamuṅ kūttum iyaṟṟat terintavarkaḷ allareṉavuṅ, kataitaḻīiya nāṭakam paṇṭaiyāriyarkkuḷ naṭaipeṟṟa teṉpataṟku āriyavēta nūlkaḷuḷ yāṇṭum ētorukuṟippuṅ kāṇappaṭavillai yeṉavum vaṭamoḻiyārāycciyil mikac ciṟantavarākiya kīt (Keith) eṉṉum āciriyar naṉkāyntu muṭivukaṭṭi yiruttalāl2 naṭaṉamum nāṭakamuṅ kūttu mellām mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭaṟintu nūlkaḷ eḻutiṉōr paṇṭait tamiḻāciriyarkaḷēyātal naṉku peṟappaṭāniṟkum. itaṉālaṉṟē toṉṟutoṭṭu iyalum icaiyum nāṭakamun tamiḻukkē uriyavātal terittuc cāṉṟōrellān tamiḻai muttamiḻ eṉṟu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟār ivvāṟē āriyattait tritam eṉṉum aṭai koṭuttu vaḻaṅkal vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭā maiyiṉ, avvaḻakkut tamiḻukkē ciṟantatātal teḷiyappaṭum eṉpatu.
allatūum, nāṭakattai mutaṟkaṭ ṭōṟṟuvitta iṟaivaṉ ataṉaip paratamuṉivaṉukkuk koṭuttu, ataṉai avaṉ vāyilāka vaḻaṅka viṭuttaṉaṉ eṉṟu nāṭṭiya cāttiram urai tarutalāl, nāṭakattiṟku mutalāciriyaṉ parataṉē yeṉpatūum naṉku peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. civapirāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vanta muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷēyātalum, avaṉait tīppiḻampoḷiyil vaittu vaṇaṅkit tīvaḷartta tamiḻ vakuppiṉar paratarē yātalum ematu māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum nūlil3 virivāka viḷakkik kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ippeṟṟiyarāṉa tamiḻpparata vakuppiṉariṟ ṟōṉṟiya paratamuṉiva ṉoruvaṉukkē civapirāṉāl nāṭakam vaḻaṅkap paṭṭa teṉṉum paṇṭai varalāṟu, paḻaiya nāṭakakkalai tamiḻmakkaḷuḷḷētāṉ tōṉṟi neṭuka vaḻaṅkivaralāyiṟ ṟeṉṉum uṇmaiyiṉai uḷḷaṅkai nellikkaṉi pōl viḷḷa viḷakkutal kaṇṭu koḷka. allatūum, iññāṉṟu vaṭamoḻikkaṇ uḷḷa nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ īṟṟil nuvalappaṭum vāḻtturaiyum paratavākkiyam eṉap peyar kūṟappaṭṭu varutalum itaṟkup piṉṉumoru cāṉṟāy nilaiyum atuvēyumaṉṟip, pācaṉ eṉṉum nāṭakappulavaṉaittavira, avaṉukkuppiṉ vantōrāṉa cūttirakaṉ kāḷitācaṉ, harṣaṉ, pavapūti mutalāṉa vaṭamoḻināṭakap pērāciriya rellārun tām iyaṟṟiya nāṭakakkāppiyaṅkaḷiṉ mutalilum iṭaiyilum īṟṟilu mellāñ civapirāṉaiyē vaṇaṅkiyum vaḻuttiyuṅ kuṟippiṭṭum iruttalum, ap perumāṉaic ciṟanteṭuttu vaḻipaṭun tamiḻp pērāciriyarkaḷē nāṭakam vallārātalai naṉkaṟivuṟuttum aṭaiyāḷamāmeṉka.
iṅṅaṉañ civapirāṉ muppuraṅkaḷai yeritta katai taḻīip paṇṭutoṭṭu naṭittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭu vanta nāṭakaṅ koṭukoṭṭi eṉṉum peyarttāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum paḻamaikkālattē naṭikkappaṭṭu vanta toṉṟām. iṟṟaikku 1700 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ aracu vīṟṟirunta ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉuñ cēravēntaṉ, taṉṉaip paḻitta vaṭanāṭṭu aracarmēṟ paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu, avarai yellām pōril veṉṟu, ciṟaippaṭuttik kaṇṇakiyiṉ uruvañ camaittataṟku imayamalaik kaṇ āynteṭutta karuṅkallai avar talaimītēṟṟik koṇarvittut, taṉ māpperun tēviyōṭum nāṭaka araṅkēṟi nāḷōlakkam irunta ñāṉṟu paṟaiyūrk kūttaccākkaiyaṉ eṉṉum nāṭakam vallāṉ, civapirāṉ muppurameritta koṭukoṭṭi, nāṭakattaiyē avar muṉṉilaiyil naṭittukkāṭṭi avarai makiḻvittaṉaṉeṉṟu, appōtu avarōṭu oruṅkiruntu ataṉaik kaṇṭa āciriyar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ.
  āṅkavaḷ taṉṉuṭaṉ aṇimaṇi araṅkam
  vīṅkunīr ñālam āḷvōṉ ēṟit
  tirunilaic cēvaṭic cilampuvāy pulampavum
  paritaru ceṅkaiyiṟ paṭupaṟai ārppavuñ
  ceṅkaṇ āyiran tirukkuṟip paruḷavuñ
  ceñcaṭai ceṉṟu ticaimukam alampavum
  pāṭakam pataiyātu cūṭakan tuḷaṅkātu
  mēkalai oliyātu meṉmulai acaiyātu
  vārkuḻai āṭātu maṇikkuḻal aviḻātu
  umaiyavaḷ orutiṟa ṉāka ōṅkiya
  imaiyavaṉ āṭiya koṭṭic cētam
  pāttaru nālvakai maṟaiyōr paṟaiyūrk
  kūttac cākkaiyaṉ āṭaliṉ makiḻntu.
(cilappatikāram, naṭukaṟkātai, 65-77)
eṉṟu aruḷicceytirukkumāṟṟāṟ, koṭukoṭṭi nāṭakan tamiḻnāṭṭiṉkaṇṇum āṭappaṭṭu vantamai naṉkaṟiyappaṭu meṉpatu.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, paṇṭaittamiḻ moḻikkaṇ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nāṭakakkāppiyaṅkaḷum, akkāppiya ilak kaṇakkūṟum nāṭakat tamiḻnūlkaḷuñ cilappatikāra urai yāciriyarāṉa aṭiyārkku nallār kālattilēyē aiyakō! iṟantu pōyiṉa! avai aṅṅaṉam iṟantupaṭṭatu etaṉāleṉiṟ kūṟutum; icai nāṭakaṅkaḷai aruvaruppārāṉa pautta camaṇcamayattavarkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟum perun tiraḷāyp pōntu ittamiḻ nāṭeṅkaṇuṅ kuṭi pukuntu vaikiya ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal, avar taṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷiṟ cikkuṇṭa tamiḻāciriyarkaḷum avarpōl avaṟṟai aruvaruttu viṭavē annūlkaḷellām aṅṅaṉam aḻintupaṭalāyiṉa! avarkkuppiṉ caivacamayattai vaḷarttu vanta āciriyarkaḷum, īraneñcam aṟṟa pautta camaṇ tuṟavoḻukkamuṟaikaḷait taḻīit tamakkup paṇṭēyuriya viḻumiya aṉpiṉaintiṇai oḻukkaviyalkaḷai orīit tāmum  avarpōl icai nāṭakat tamiḻaik kainnekiḻa viṭṭaṉar! iṅṅaṉam tam nāṭṭuṭpukum ayalavar oḻukalāṟukaḷ ettiṟattavāy iruppiṉum avai  tammai ārāyātu kaippaṟṟit, tamakkuriya cīrtta paṇṭaiyurimaikaḷai yellām eḷitil iḻantu viṭutaṟkaṇ muṉ niṟpā rāṉa nan tamiḻmakkaḷiṭaiyē icaināṭakat tamiḻ iṟantupaṭṭatu iyalpēyaṉṟō? icai nāṭakam maṭṭumō! iyaṟṟamiḻ naṅkaiyuṅkūṭat taṉ taṉicciṟappai varavara iḻantu, ayalmoḻic coṟporuḷ nañcu taṉ kaṇ aḷaviṉṟi viravappeṟṟuk kuṟaiyuyiruṭaiyaḷāyt, taṉṉai muṉpōl arukāp pēriḷamaiyiṟ peruka vaikkum ōr āṇmaip perumakaṉ utaviyai vēṇṭiniṟṟal aṟiyātār yār? atuniṟka.
iṉit, teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭavar nilai ivvāṟāyiṉum, vaṭanāṭṭil āriyamoḻiyait taḻīi niṉṟa nam muṉṉōr nilai attuṇai iraṅkat takkatāyillai. vaṭanāṭṭilirunta aṟiñar āriyamoḻiyai vaḻaṅkap pukuntiṭiṉun, tamakkuriya icai nāṭakaṅkaḷaiyum orē teyvak kōṭpāṭṭaiyuṅ kaiviṭārāy, avai tammai mutalvaittē iyal icai nāṭaka nūlkaḷuñ camaya ārāycci nūlkaḷum piṟavum vaṭa moḻikkaṭ ṭoṭarpāka iyaṟṟi varalāṉār. eṉṟivvāṟu vaṭamoḻik kaṇ nāṭakanūl vanta varalāṟṟiṉ uṇmai aṟintukoḷka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	immuṭivai ematu māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum nūliṉ 729ām pakkam mutaṟ kāṇka.
2.	Read Dr. A. B. Keith’s ‘The Sanscrit Drama, pp.25-27.
3.	māṇikkavācakar kālam pakkam, 16 164 - 67.	
3. nāṭaka amaippu
iṉi, nampōṉṟa makkaḷuṭ cilar tamatiyaṟkaikkun tamatu collukkun tamatu oḻukalāṟṟiṟkun tamatu iṉpa nukarccikkum ottu niṟpārōṭu uṟavukoṇṭum, avaṟṟukku māṟupaṭṭu niṟpāroṭu pakaikoṇṭum oḻukāniṟpuḻi, avartam vāḻnāḷ nikaḻccikaḷ oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu piṇaintu cikkuṇṭu, īṟṟil ac cikku viṭupaṭṭu naṉṟāy muṭitaluñ, cikku viṭupaṭātu tītāy muṭitaluṅ kāṇṭu maṉṟē. iṅṅaṉam iruvēṟu vakaippaṭṭu muṭiyum makkaḷiyaṟkai nikaḻccikaḷiṉ toṭarpē kataiyeṉṉum peyarttāy nāṭaka araṅkiṉkaṇ vaittu āṭuvārāl naṭattik kāṭṭappaṭuvatākum. eṉavē, nāṭakattiṟku uyirāvatu oru ciṟanta kataiyēyā meṉpatūum, akkataiyiṉ kaṭ ṭoṭarpupaṭṭu niṟkum makkaḷaiyum makkaḷ nikaḻccikaḷaiyum nāṭaka araṅkiṉkaṭpōntu āṭikkāṭṭu vār aṅṅaṉam āṭikkāṭṭutaṟku icainta muṟaiyāl ellām vaittu oru nallicaip pulavaṉāl toṭukkap paṭuvatē nāṭakakkāppiyamā meṉpatūum iṉitu peṟappaṭum.
aṟṟēl, katainūlukkum nāṭaka nūlukkum vēṟupāṭu eṉṉaiyeṉiṟ, katainūl eḻutum āciriyaṉ taṉ uraiyākak kūṟavēṇṭuvaṉa vellāṅ kūṟutaṟku uriyaṉ, maṟṟu, nāṭaka nūlāciriyaṉō, nāṭakakkataiyuḷ varuvār peyarum, avar varutalpōtalum, avarvarum iṭamuṅkālamuṅ kuṟippataṉṟi, aṅṅaṉam varuvār iyaṟkai ceyaṟkaikaḷum avar varutalpōtal vakaikaḷum avarvarum iṭaṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟkai kālattiṉ nilaikaḷun tāṉē nērniṉṟu virittuk kūṟutaṟku uriyāṉ allaṉ aṉṟi avaikaḷaik kūṟa viḻaikuvāṉēl, tāṉ iyaṟṟum nāṭakakkataiyil varuvār vāyilniṉṟē avai tammaic curukkamāka varuvippāṉallatu, tāṉē avai tammaik kūṟappeṟāṉ, katainūlāciriyaṉō tāṉ varaiyuṅ kataiyuḷ varuvāriṉ vēṟākat taṉṉait taṉiniṟuttit, taṉakkumuṉ celvārāka avarait taṉṉetir toṭarpupaṭa niṟutti avartam iyaṟkai ceyaṟkaikaḷaiyum avartam varutal pōtal  vakaikaḷaiyum avar iyaṅkum iṭattiṉiyaṟkai kālattiṉ nilaikaḷaiyum tāṉ vēṇṭumā ṟellām virittēyāyiṉuñ curukkiyē yāyiṉum varaiyappeṟuvaṉ. mēlum, oru nāṭakakkatai nikaḻcciyāṉatu ōr iravil oṉpatu maṇikkut tuvaṅki iraṇṭallatu mūṉṟumaṇikkuḷ naṭattik kāṭṭutaṟku icaintatāka irukkavēṇṭumātalāl, atu virintu cellutaṟku iṭampeṟātu; aṉṟiyum. mika virinta pāratak katai irāmāyaṇak katai kantapurāṇak katai mutaliyavaṟṟai nāṭakamāka naṭattik kāṭṭappukuvārum, avai tammaip pala pakutikaḷākap pirittu, av vovvoru pakutiyaiyum ovvōriravil muṭikkattakka paṟpala nāṭakaṅkaḷāka iyaṟṟi naṭattik kāṭṭak kāṇṭumākaliṉ, nāṭakameṉa vakukkappaṭuva vellām ōriravil naṭattik kāṭṭutaṟku ēṟṟa curuṅkiya aḷavuṭaiyatākavē iruttal vēṇṭumeṉ ṟuṇarntu koḷka. ivvaḷavu kaṭantu iyaṟṟappaṭuvaṉa nāṭakamuṟaiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭālum, avai ōriravil āṭik kāṭṭutaṟku icaiyāmaiyiṉ nāṭakamātal cellāteṉka. maṟṟuk, katainūṟkataikaḷō aṅṅaṉam āṭik kāṭṭappaṭuvaṉa allavāy, ettaṉai nāṭkaḷēṉun toṭarntu payilutaṟkēṟṟa amaippuṭaiya vākaliṉ, avai āciriyaṉ vēṇṭu māṟellām virintu cellutaṟ kēṟṟa peṟṟiyavā meṉṟu kaṭaippiṭikka.
iṉṉum, nāṭakam eṉpatu parantupaṭṭa in nilavulakattiṉ oru pakutiyilō aṉṟic cila pakutiyilō naṭaipeṟum makkaḷ cilar palariṉ iyakkat toṭarpai varai yaṟaippaṭṭa oru ciṟiya iṭamākiya araṅkiṉ kaṇṇē aḻaku mikuttu āṭikkāṭṭun tiṟaṉuṭaiyatu; attiṟattiṉai niṉaintu pārkkuṅkāl, oru pulnuṉimēl niṉṟa oru ciṟupaṉit tivalaiyāṉatu taṉṉaic cūḻavuḷḷa periya iyaṟkaipporuṭ ṭōṟṟaṅkaḷait taṉṉakat taṭakkik kāṭṭun tiṟattiṉaiyē ataṟku oppākac collalām. maṟṟu, virinta oru katainūlō aṅṅaṉamē virinta makkaḷiyal nikaḻccikaḷait taṉkaṇ nīḷakkāṭṭun taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu ataṟku mika akaṉṟatōr aḻakiya ērinīr, taṉakkumēl muḻunilā vaṭṭamiṭṭu vayaṅka ataṉaic cūḻa vāṉmīṉ ṟokutikaḷ poṉṟukaḷeṉa miḷirat tōṉṟum oru peru nīla vāṉparappait taṉkaṇ viḷaṅkak kāṭṭutalaiyē uvamaiyākac collalām. ivvāṟu avaiyiraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumai yuṇarntu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
iṉip, paṇṭaināḷil nāṭakaṅkaḷ ceyyuḷum uraiyum viraviya naṭaiyil ākkappaṭṭaṉa. maṟṟuk, katainūlkaḷō ceyyuḷnaṭai oṉṟilēyē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭakaṅkaḷ ēṉ aṅṅaṉañ ceyyuḷum uraiyuṅ kalanta naṭaiyil ākkap paṭṭaṉa veṉiṉ avai, ulakattil nikaḻum makkaḷ oḻukalāṟu kaḷaip perumpālum aṅku nikaḻumāṟē yeṭuttu āṭik kāṭṭa vallārāl ōr araṅkiṉkaṇ vaittu naṭattik kāṭṭappaṭun taṉmaiyavātalāl, ulakattiṟ kalviyaṟivilum āṟṟalilum mikka mēṉmakkaḷiṉ urai yāṭṭukaḷaic ceyyuḷ naṭaiyilum, avarallāta ēṉai makkaḷiṉ uraiyāṭṭukaḷai avaravar uyarvu tāḻvukkēṟṟa urainaṭaiyilumāka iyaṟkaikku māṟākāmal naṭattik kāṭṭiṉāl maṭṭumē avaṟṟai nēriruntu kāṇpārkku iṉpam uṇṭām; ākavē, nāṭakaṅkaḷellām iyaṟkaineṟi tiṟampāmal aṅṅaṉam iruvakai naṭaiyum oruṅkuvirāy amaikkappaṭalāyiṉa. maṟṟuk, kataikaḷai nuvaluṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷō, araṅkiṉkaṇ vaittu avai tammai naṭattik kāṭṭuvaṉa allavāy, iyaṟkai ceyaṟkaikaḷai yeṭuttu virikkun tam āciriyaṉṟaṉ aḵkiyakaṉṟa aṟiviṉṟiṟaṅkaḷaiyē perumpāṉmaiyum pulappaṭuttuvaṉavāyt, tammai ūṉṟip payilvārkkuc coṟcuvai poruṭcuvaikaḷaip perukuvikkum peṟṟiya vākaliṉ, avaitam iyalukku ēṟpac ceyyuḷ naṭaivaḷam oṉṟē poruntalāyiṉa veṉka. eṉavē, kāḷitācarum avarkku muṉ, piṉ ṉirunta nāṭaka āciriyaru mellān tām toṭutta nāṭakakkataiyuḷ varuvār uyarvu tāḻvukkēṟṟa ceyyuḷnaṭai urainaṭaikaḷ viraviya muṟaiyiṟ ṟammuṭaiya nāṭakanūlkaḷai iyaṟṟiṉārāka, viyācarum vāṉmīkiyum avarpōṉṟa ēṉaik kāppiya nūlāciriyaru mellān tām nuvaluṅ kataikaḷait tam aṟiviṉ ṟiṟaṉāl ettuṇaic cuvai peruka nuvalalāmō attuṇaikkēṟpa muḻutuñ ceyyuḷ naṭaiyilēyē yākkalāyiṉā reṉṟuṇarntukoḷka kāḷitācarum nāṭakamuṟai viṭuttuk, kāppiyamuṟai taḻīik katai nūl yākkap pukkuḻic, ceyyuḷnaṭai yoṉṟaṉaiyē mēṟkoṇṭār eṉpataṟku, avariyaṟṟiya irakuvamcam, kumāracampavam mutaliya poruṭṭoṭarnilaic ceyyuṭkaḷē cāṉṟā meṉpatu. 
4. nāṭakak kataiyamaippu
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam amaikkappaṭum nāṭakam eṉṉum ōr aḻakiya uṭampukku uyirpōlvatu, ataṉūṭu toṭarntu nikaḻuṅ kataiyē yākum. valla ōr uyir illāta veṟṟuṭampu ētoru payaṉum uṭaiyatākāmai pōlak, kataiccuvai nirampāta nāṭakamum payaṉuṭaiya tākātu ēṉeṉṟāṟ, kataiccuvai vāyāta nāṭakattaik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟuvār ulakil evarumē ilarākaliṉeṉpatu.
aḵtokkum; kataiccuvai yeṉpatu iṉṉateṉ ṟaṟintilamākaliṉ, ataṉai oruciṟitu viḷakkik kāṭṭuka veṉiṟ, kāṭṭutum. ōr araciḷaiñaṉ, taṉ nakarattiṟkup puṟampēyuḷḷa kāṉakattil ulavum puli karaṭi muḷḷampaṉṟi yāṉai mutalāṉa maṟavilaṅkukaḷ taṉkīḻ vāḻuṅ kuṭimakkaṭkut tīṅku iḻaiyāvāṟu, tiṅkaḷukku orukāl taṉ ēvalāḷar cilaruṭaṉ paṭaikkalan tāṅkik kutirai yūrntuceṉṟu, ataṉkaṇ vēṭṭamāṭit tirumpuvāṉ eṉṟa aḷavumaṭṭum oru ciṟukatai oruvar colvarāyiṉ ataṉaik kēṭpār ciṟitum iṉpuṟār. maṟṟu, ataṉai avvāṟu kūṟātu, aṅṅaṉam vēṭṭam āṭit tirumpum vaḻakkam uṭaiya avviḷaiñaṉ orukāl ak kāṉakattiṉūṭu koṭiyatoru muḷḷam paṉṟiyaip piṉṟoṭarntu tāṉ ivarnta kutiraiyai viraintu celuttic celvuḻit, taṉṉuṭaṉ pōnta ēvalāḷaraip pirintu, ataṉpiṉṉē akkāṭṭiṉūṭu neṭuntolaivu pōyviṭṭāṉ. avaṉmuṟ ceṉṟa paṉṟiyō avaṉ kaṇkaḷukkum eṭṭāmal aṭarnta putarkaḷiṉūṭu maṟaintōṭiviṭṭatu. annēram pakalavaṉ vāṉ ucciyil eṟikkum vēṉil naṇpakal, tāṉun taṉkutiraiyum neṭuvaḻi vantamaiyāl mey viyirttu nāvaṟaṇṭu kaḷaippuṟṟu, mēṟcella māṭṭāmal ōriṭattil ayarntu niṟkavēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatāyiṟṟu. uṭaṉē, avaṉ taṉ kutiraiyaiviṭṭuk kīḻiṟaṅki, ataṉai aruku ōṭiya malai nīraruviyil nīr parukkik karaiyiṟ pulmēyaviṭṭut, tāṉun taṉatu viṭāy taṇittu oru maranīḻalil amarntāṉ. amarntāṉukku ayarviṟai kaṇṇuṟakkam vara aṅkuk koḻumaiyāy vaḷarntirunta pulliṉmēṟ paṭuttāṉ; paṭuttuc ciṟitu nērattilellān taṉṉai maṟantu tuyilvāṉāyiṉaṉ ivaṉ ivvāṟirukka, ak kāṉakattil ulavuṅ koṭiya vēṅkaippuli yoṉṟu iraitēṭi ap pakkamāy vantatu. aṅkē pulmēyuṅ koḻuviya ak kutiraiyaik kaṇṭu, ataṉaik koṉṟu tiṉṟaṟku vāyait tiṟantavāṟāy ataṉmēṟ pāya muyalkaiyil, ak kutirai verukkoṇṭu ōṭit, tuyiṉṟu koṇṭirunta taṉ araciḷaiñaṉ arukē cārntu kaṉaikka, avaṉuṅ katumeṉa viḻitteḻuntu niṉṟu cūḻa nōkkiṉāṉ. itaṟkuḷ appuli taṉ muyaṟcikku iṭaiyūṟu viḷaippavaṉāy eḻuntuniṉṟa av viḷaiñaṉaik kāṇṭalun, tāṉ ak kutiraimēṟ pāyvatu viṭuttu, av viḷaiñaṉmēṟ pāyntatu. aḵtaṅṅaṉam pāytalaiyun, tuyiṉṟeḻunta taṉ talaivaṉ taṉatu vēṟpaṭai nilattiṟ kiṭappat tāṉ veṟuṅ kaiyaṉāy niṟṟalaiyuṅ kaṇṭa ak kutirai ōr imaip poḻutil taṉ ṟalaivaṟkum avaṉ mēṟ pāyum appulikkum iṭaiyē pāyntatu. itu kaṇṭa iḷaiñaṉ oru kutiyiṟ kīḻkkiṭanta taṉatu vēṟpaṭaiyaik kuṉinteṭuttu, ataṉai mikavun tiṟamaiyākac cuḻaṟṟi ap puliyiṉ mukattiṉmēl ōcci, ataṉaik koṉṟu vīḻttiṉāṉ. innērattil avaṉ ṟaṉ ēvalāḷarum avaṉpāl vantu cērntu akkutiraiyiṉ naṉṟi mikka āṇmaiyiṉaiyun, tam araciḷaiñaṉ ṟaṉ pērāṇmai yiṉaiyum viyantu, vīḻttappaṭṭa ap peruvēṅkaip puliyiṉaiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu, ellārumāyt tam nakarukkut tirumpiṉar eṉṟu av varaciḷaiñaṟku orunāḷ nikaḻnta viyattaku nikaḻcciyiṉai yeṭuttuk kūṟiṉāl, ataṉaik kēṭpārkku, naṉṟiyun tikaippum irakkamum accamum aiyamum muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkaiyūm viyappum makiḻcciyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākat tōṉṟi avaraip peritum iṉpuṟuttumaṉṟō, yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ, kuṭi makkaḷaip pātukākkum oru nalla aracaṟkup putalvaṉāy vaḷaviya celvavāḻkkaiyil uṟaiyum ōr araciḷaiñaṉ taṉ nalattaik karutātu taṉ kuṭimakkaḷ nalattaiyē karuti maṟavilaṅkuḷai vēṭṭamāṭac ceṉṟā ṉeṉpatu kēṭṭu naṉṟiyuṇarvum, aṅṅaṉañ ceṉṟavaṉ accantarattakka akkāṉakattiṉūṭu taṉ ēvalāḷaraiyum pirintu taṉiyē cellumāṟu nērntatu eṉpatu kēṭṭut tikaip puṇarvum, orunāḷum nīrviṭāyum pacittuṉpamuṅ kaḷaippum aṟiyā tāṉ kaṭuvēṉil naṇpakalil ak kāṭṭiṟ ṟaṉicceṉṟu viṭāyttu varuntiṉāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭu irakkavuṇarvum, ayarntuṟaṅkum av viḷaiñaṟku aṇittāka maṟavēṅkai oṉṟu vantateṉpatu kēṭṭu accavuṇarvum, avvēṅkai kutiraimēṟ pāytaloḻintu av viḷaiñaṉmēṟ pāyap pukka teṉpatu kēṭṭu iḵtu yātāy muṭiyumō eṉṉum aiyavuṇarvum, iḵteṅṅaṉam muṭiva tāyiṟṟeṉa aṟiyaviḻaiyum vēṭkaiyuṇarvum, av vēṅkai taṉ talaivaṉ mēṟ pāyap pukuvatu kaṇṭa ak kutirai taṉakku varum ūṟupāṭṭaiyum oru poruṭpaṭuttātu avaṟkum ataṟkum iṭaiyē pāyntamai kēṭṭum annoṭiyil avviḷaiñaṉ taṉ vēṟpaṭaiyai eṭuttōcci ap puliyaik koṉṟu vīḻttamai kēṭṭum viyappoṭu kalanta makiḻcci yuṇarvum, av viḷaiñaṉaip pirinta ēvalar avaṉpāl vantu cērntu ellārumāyt tamatu nakarkku iṉitupōyc cērntamai kēṭṭu āṟutal kalanta makiḻcciyuṇarvum oṉṟaṉ piṉṉoṉṟāyt tōṉṟi ikkatai kēṭpāraip peritum iṉpuṟuttukiṉṟamaiyi ṉeṉpatu. ivvāṟu oru katai nikaḻcci ciṟitāyiruppiṉum peritā yiruppiṉun taṉṉaik kēṭpārkku mēṟkāṭṭiya palvakai yuṇarvukaḷai eḻuppun takaittāy amaikkappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ tām aruḷicceyta tirucciṟṟampalakkōvai yāril mēl uraitta ciṟukataiyait,
   tīvāy uḻuvai kiḻittatantō ciṟitē piḻaippit
  tāvāmaṇivēl paṇikoṇṭavāṟiṉṟor āṇṭakaiyē
eṉṟu aḵkiya aṟivāl iraṇṭaṭiyiṟ ṟiṟampaṭak kuṟittu aṟivuṟuttaruḷiṉamai kaṇṭu iṟumpūteytuka. eṉavē, oruvaṉ allatu oruttiyatu oḻukalāṟu ētōr iṭai yūṟṟāṉun taṭaippaṭātu ceṉṟu muṭiyumāyiṉ ataṉai   eṭutturaippatum ataṉaik kēṭpatuṅ kūṟuvārkkuṅ kēṭpārkkum viyappiṉāl nikaḻum iṉpavuṇarviṉaip payavāmaiyiṉ aḵtu oru kataiyākat toṭukkaṟpāla taṉṟeṉpatūum, avvāṟaṉṟi iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl tākkuṇṭu naṉṟākavō tītākavō muṭiyum oruvaratu oḻukalāṟē irutiṟattārkkum viyappiṉaiyum iṉpavuṇarviṉaiyun tantu kataiyākat toṭukkappaṭutaṟkurittāmeṉpatūum iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkumeṉka.
aṟṟēl, aṅṅaṉam iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl tākkuṇṇum oruvaratu oḻukalāṟu maṭṭumē viyappiṉaiyum iṉpat tiṉaiyun tarutal eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ ōr yāṟṟu veḷḷattiṉ viraivum viraiviṉmaiyum valivum valiviṉmaiyum ataṉaik kuṟukkiṭṭu niṟkum aṇaiyiṉ uraṅkoṇṭē aṟiyap paṭutalpōlavum, oru neruppiṉ vaṉmaiyum meṉmaiyum ataṉāl erikkappaṭum oru kāṉakattiṉ aḷavupaṟṟiyē uṇarappaṭutalpōlavum, oru cūṟaikkāṟṟiṉ kaṭumaiyun taṇivum ataṉāl, alaittu muṟikkappaṭum maraṅkaḷiṉ nilaipaṟṟiyē aḷantaṟiyappaṭutal pōlavum oruvaruṭaiya aṟivāṟṟal uṭalāṟṟalum maṉanalamum avaiyiṉmaiyu mellām avaratu oḻukalāṟṟai vantu iṭaimaṟikkum iṭai yūṟukaḷiṉ vaṉmai meṉmai vāyilākavē naṉkaḷantaṟiyap paṭumeṉka. aṅṅaṉam vantu maṟikkum iṭaiyūṟukaḷukkum añcātu avaṟṟai mēṟkaṭantu celvāṉṟaṉ āṇmai yiṉaik kaṇṭu viyantu makiḻtalum, maṟṟu avviṭai yūṟukaḷai etirntum avaṟṟai mēṟkoḷa māṭṭātu ayarntu māḻkuvāṉaik kaṇṭu iraṅki varuntutalum, iṉi avai tammaik kaṇṭu añciyōṭu vāṉaik kaṇṭu iḻittu ikaḻtalum makkaḷ ellār māṭṭum iyaṟkaiyāy nikaḻak kāṇṭumaṉṟē. ākavē, at taṉmaiyavām iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl tākkappaṭātavaṉṟaṉ uramum uramiṉyum aṟitaṟku vēṟu viḻumiya vāyil oṉṟuṅ kāṇappaṭāmaiyiṉ, ap peṟṟiyāṉṟaṉ oḻukalāṟu makkaḷevarkkum viḻumitām iṉpam payavāmaiyum iyaṟkaiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟṟāṟ, katai kaḷeṉṟaṟkuc ciṟantaṉa iṭaiyūṟṟiṉ vāyilāka oruvaṉṟaṉ allatu oruttitaṉ uḷḷa nilaiyiṉaip pulappaṭutti, avvāṟṟāl ataṉaip payilvārkkuṅ kāṇpārkkuṅ kēṭpārkkum iṉpa vuṇarviṉai eḻuppum nīravā meṉṟu teḷintu koḷka.
iṉi, iccākuntala nākaṭattiṉkaṇ nuvalappaṭuṅ katai nikaḻcciyum iṭaiyiṭaiyē vantu kuṟukkiṭun taṭaikaḷāl annikaḻccikkuriyāriṉ iyaṟkai nilaikaḷaip pulappaṭutti avaṟṟai yuṇarvārkku accamum viyappum muṭivaṟi vēṭkaiyum makiḻcciyum payantu avarai iṉpuṟuttumāṟu oruciṟitu kāṭṭuvām. tuṣiyantavēntaṉ tērilamarntu villuṅkaṇaiyum ēnti oru māṉaip piṉṟoṭarnta vaṇṇamāy oru kāṉakattiṉūṭu viraintu celkiṉ ṟuḻit tuṟavōr cilar iṭaiyē pukuntu ammāṉiṉaik kolla lākāteṉṟu maṟikka, aracaṉum avaratu kaṭṭaḷaikkuk kīḻppaṭintu ataṉaik kollātu viṭṭu viṭukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl, aracaṉ ōruyiraik kolla muṉaintu niṉṟavaḻiyum, aṟavōr collukku aṭaṅki ataṉai avvāṟu ceyyātu viṭṭa maṉanalaṉ uṭaiya ṉātal pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? iṉṉum, naṭuvē pukunta tuṟavōr, mēl naṭaipeṟavēṇṭuṅ katai nikaḻccikkum vaḻi ceykunarāy niṟṟalum aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu; yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, avarkaḷ avvaracaṉaik kaṇṇuvar āciramattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkē cakuntalaiyāl viruntēṟkap paṭumāṟu ēvukiṉṟamaiyiṉeṉpatu.
iṉi, avvāciramattiṟkuḷ nuḻainta avvaracaṉ taṉakkuc ciṟitu tolaiviṟ cakuntalaiyum avaṭaṉ tōḻi māruṅ kuṭaṅkaḷil mukanta nīrai iḷañceṭikaḷukku viṭṭuk koṇṭu uraiyāṭi varutalaiyum, avarkaḷ aḻakil mikkavarāy iruttalaiyuṅ kaṇṭu, avarkaḷpāl uṭaṉē cellutaṟku māṭṭāṉāy, maracceṟivukaḷiṉ maṟaivil niṉṟapaṭiyāy avarkaḷai nōkki avaruraiyāṭṭukaḷaiyuṅ kēṭkiṉṟāṉ. innērattil oru vaṇṭu cakuntalaiyiṉ mukattaṇṭai paṟantu vantu avaḷait tuṉpuṟuttā niṟka, attuṉpattiṉiṉṟu taṉṉai viṭuvikkumāṟu avaḷ taṉ tōḻimārkaḷai vēṇṭiyaḻaikkiṉṟāḷ. tōḻimārō, tamatu nāṭṭukku aracaṉāṉa tuṣiyantaṉ aṇṭaiyil iruttalai aṟiyārā yiruntum, pakaṭikkāka nammaiyellāṅ kākkum aracaṉ tuṣiyantaṉaik kūppiṭu! eṉkiṉṟaṉar; eṉṟatum, maṟaivi lirunta aracaṉ taṉṉait terivittuk koḷḷutaṟku atutāṉ takkanērameṉak karuti avvaṇṭai ōccuvāṉpōl ataṉai ataṭṭiya vaṇṇamāy avarmaruṅku aṇaikiṉṟāṉ. pārmiṉkaḷ aracaṉatu iyakkattiṟkut taṭaiyāyp pukunta tuṟavōr avaṟkuc cakuntalaiyaik kāṭṭiṉar cakuntalaiyiṉ iyakkattiṟkut taṭaiyāyp pukunta oru vaṇṭu avaṭku aracaṉaik kāṭṭiṟṟu. iṉṉum, piṉ nikaḻnta taṭaiyāṉatu cakuntalai ciṟituvarun tuṉpattiṟkum añci veruḷum meṉṟaṉmai yuṭaiya ḷeṉpataṉaiyum, ittuṇai melliyal puṭaiyāḷ iṉit taṉ kaṇavaṉāl taṉakku viḷaiyun tuṉpattai eṅṅaṉan tāṅkuvāḷeṉpa taṉaiyum aṟivittal kāṇka. ivviruvakait taṭaikaḷāl iṭai maṟikkappaṭṭuc cellum ikkatai nikaḻcci, taṉṉaip payilvārkku viyappu, muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkai, irakkam mutaliya vuṇarvukaḷaik kiḷaṟi makiḻcci ceytal uṇarntukoḷka.
iṉṉum, aracaṉ mēṟ colliyavāṟu cakuntalaiyiṉ tōḻimārōṭu uraiyāṭikkoṇṭu tāṉ cakuntalai mēṟkoṇṭa kātalaip pulappaṭuttiyuñ, cakuntalai taṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kātalai uyttaṟintun tāṉum avarum iṉpuṟṟu niṟkaiyil, akkāṉakattiṉūṭu oru kāṭṭiyāṉai ataṉai yuḻakkikkoṇṭu varutalai yuṇarntu aṅku iyaṅkuvār iṭum peruntikilōcai avaratu nēya nikaḻccikku iṭaiyūṟāyp pukuntu? ammakaḷiraiyum avvaracaṉaiyum vēṟupirittu, itaṉaip payilvārkku accattiṉaiyum, piṉ ikkatainikaḻcci yātāy muṭiyumō eṉa muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkaiyiṉaiyum viḷaivittu niṟṟa laṟintu koḷka.
mēlum, aṅṅaṉañ cakuntalaiyaic caṭutiyiṟ pirintu taṉ kūṭārattuṭ ceṉṟuvaiki avaḷvayappaṭṭa niṉaiviṉaṉāy, maṟittum avaḷpāṟ cellutaṟku vāyileṉṉai yeṉṟu ārāyntu varuntikkoṇṭirukkum aracaṉpāl, mīṇṭum avvāciramattiluḷḷa tuṟavikaḷ cilarpōntu, kaṇṇuva muṉivar varumvaraiyil tāmvēṭkum vēḷvikaṭku arakkarāl iṭukkaṇ uṇṭākātavāṟu avaṟṟaip pātukākkaveṉṟu vēṇṭa, aracaṉum ataṟkuṭaṉē iyaintu, mīḷat tāṉ cakuntalaipāṟ cellutaṟku ataṉaiyē ēṟṟatoru vāyilākap paṟṟutal kāṇka. mīṇṭum nērnta ittuṟavōr vēṇṭukōḷ, iṭaiyaṟunta maṇikkōvaip poṟ caraṭṭiṉai iṇaikkum poṉ ācupōl, iṭai viṭṭa ikkatai nikaḻcciyiṉait toṭarpu paṭuttutaliṉ, iḵtu itaṉ muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkai kiḷarntārkkup perumakiḻcciyiṉaip payattal tērntukoḷka.
iṉṉun, tuṟavōriṉ vēṇṭukōḷukku iṇaṅki aracaṉ mīṇṭuṅ kaṇṇuvaratu pāḻikku (āciramattiṟku)c cellat tuvaṅkukaiyil, aracaṉṟaṉ aṉṉai taṉatu nōṉpu muṭiviṉkaṇ taṉṉōṭu uṭaṉirukkumāṟu aracaṉaik kālan tāḻātu nakarukku mīḷka eṉak kaṭṭaḷai viṭuttamai, piṉṉum aracaṟkōr iṭaiyūṟāyp pukutal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam maṟittum pukunta iṭaiyūṟṟai aracaṉ taṉ naṇpaṉākiya vitūṣakaṉai nakarkkup pōkku māṟṟal nīkkiviṭutaliṉ, ituvuṅ katai nikaḻcciyiṉai mīṇṭun toṭarpupaṭuttuṅ karuviyāyp payilvārai iṉpuṟuttutal aṟintukoḷka.
atumēyumaṉṟi, iraṇṭāmvakuppiṉ īṟṟil, in nāṭaka  āciriyaṉ mēl varuṅ katai nikaḻccikku oruperuṅ kāraṇamākat tantirukkuṅ kuṟippum peritum viyakkattakkatā yirukkiṉṟatu. mēlē, cakuntalaiyai muṟṟum maṟantuviṭṭa aracaṉpāṟ, kaṇṇuvarāl viṭukkappaṭṭuc ceṉṟa avaḷ, avaṉ taṉṉaik kāṉakattil maṇanta varalāṟukaḷai yellām ettuṇaiyō eṭutturaittum, avaṉ avaikaḷai niṉaivu kūrarkkillāṉāy avaḷai veṟuttu otukkiya piṟ cilanāḷiṟ kiṭaitta taṉatu kaṇaiyāḻiyaik kaṇṭu, avaḷait  tāṉ maṇanta varalāṟukaḷaiyellām niṉaivukūrntu taṉ nēyaṉākiya vitūṣakaṉ avvaralāṟukaḷait taṉakku niṉaivūṭṭāmai eṉṉai yeṉṟu avaṉai varuntik kēṭṭalum, avvitūṣakaṉ uṇmaiyil eṉakku attuṟavi makaḷiṭattil evvakaiyāṉa viruppamum illai. *** nāṉ pakaṭiyāyc coṉṉa coṟkaḷai uṇmaiyāka niṉaiyātē eṉṟu aracaṉ iraṇṭām vakuppiṉ īṟṟilē kāṉakattiṟ ṟaṉakkuc colliya coṟkaḷai eṭuttu moḻintamai kāṇka. ākavē, mēlnikaḻuṅ katainikaḻcci yiṉai muṉṉuṇarntu, ataṟkutaviyāka aracaṉ vāymoḻiyil vaittuk kāḷitācar iraṇṭām vakuppiṉ īṟṟiṟ kūṟiya coṟkaḷiṉ payaṉ peritum niṉaivukūrntu viyakkaṟpālatā meṉka. 
iṉṉum, aracaṉ cakuntalaiyai maṇantu cilanāḷ vaikip, piṉṉart taṉ nakarkku ēki, avaḷait taṉpāl aḻaittukkoḷḷa viḻaintavaṉ, atu ceyyātu avaḷai muṟṟumē maṟantupōy viṭṭāṉ. ivvaḷavil innāṭakam niṉṟu viṭumāyiṉ, aracaṉ avaḷai aṅṅaṉam aṟavē maṟantupōyatu eṉṉai yeṉṟum, avaṉāl aṅṅaṉaṅ kaiviṭappaṭṭa cakuntalaiyiṉ vāḻkkai piṉṉar evvāṟu āyiṟ ṟeṉṟum aṟiya māṭṭāmaiyiṟ payilvōr uḷḷam amaitipeṟātu ataṉāl innāṭakañ cuvaikuṉṟik kuṟaipāṭu uṭaiyatākum. ākavē, āciriyar kāḷitācar, cakuntalai māṭṭup peruṅkātal koṇṭavaṉākiya avvaracaṉ avaḷai aṅṅaṉam eḷitilē maṟantuviṭutal iyalāmai yuṇarntu, avaṉ avaḷai muṟṟum maṟantu pōtaṟku oruperuṅkāraṇaṅ kaṟpippārāyt, turuvācar cāpattai innāṭakattiṉ nāṉkām vakuppiṉ tuvakkat tilēyē varuvittal kāṇka. taṉ āruyirk kātalaṉaiyē niṉaintu avaṉvayamākiya uḷḷattiṉaḷāyt, taṉṉaiyun taṉṉaiccūḻa  nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷaiyum oruṅkēmaṟantu, ayarntu taṉkuṭil vāyilil amarntirunta cakuntalai, caṭutiyil viruntiṉarāyp pōnta turuvācariṉ varukaiyaiyum uṇarātiruppavē, avar avaḷmēṟ cīṟṟaṅkoṇṭu niṉmaṉam evaṉvayappaṭṭu evaṉaiyē niṉaintu koṇṭirukkiṉṟatō, avaṉ *** niṉaivūṭṭap paṭṭālum niṉṉai niṉaiyā toḻika eṉṟu vacai (cāpam) kūṟic ceṉṟa oru nikaḻcciyiṉai āciriyar īṇṭup paṭaittu moḻintamaiyum, ivvaḷavilum ak katai nikaḻcciyiṉai aṟuttu niṟuttāmal, niṉaivu kūrtaṟku aṭaiyāḷamāṉa ōr aṇikalattaik kāṇṭalum avvacavu nīṅkum eṉṟu atu tīrtaṟkum vaḻi ceytu, cakuntalai mīṇṭun taṉ kātalaṉaik kūṭuvatākiya nikaḻcci yiṉait toṭarpupaṭuttu vaittamaiyumākiya aruntiṟaṉ peritum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatoṉṟāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
atuvēyumaṉṟi, mēṟ coṉṉavāṟu nērnta turuvācar vacaiyiṉaic cakuntalai aṟivaḷāyiṉ, avaḷ taṉ kaiyil aracaṉ aṇinta kaṇaiyāḻiyiṉpāṟ karuttu mika vuṭaiyaḷāy atu taṉ kaiyaiviṭṭu naḻuvāmaṟ pātukāttu oḻuku vaḷākaliṉ, aracaṉ avaḷai maṟattalum nērātu. atu nērātākavē, mēṟ katai nikaḻcciyuñ, cakuntalaiyiṉ maṉat tiṭpattaiyuṅ kaṟpiṉ kaṭappāṭṭaiyum ūṭuruvi oḷira māṭṭātāyc cuvaikuṉṟi yoḻiyum. ākavē, āciriyar kāḷitācar attuṇaik kuṟaipāṭukaḷum itaṉkaṇ ēṟāmai vilakkutaṟ poruṭṭu īṇṭucceyta ceykaittiṟam nuṭpam niṉaiyun tōṟum pēruvakaiyiṉai ūṭṭāniṟkiṉṟatu. turuvācar varukaiyiṉaiyum, avar kūṟiya vacaimoḻi yiṉaiyum aṟinta tōḻimār, avaitammai aṟiyātirunta cakuntalaikku avaṟṟai aṟiviyāmalē viṭumāṟu ceyta āciriyaratu nuṭpa viṉaittiṟaṉ, kataiyiṉ ṟoṭarpai uḷ nuḻaintu kaṇṭu iṇaikkamāṭṭārkku viḷaṅkātāyiṉum, ataṉai avvāṟu kaṇṭu toṭukka vallārkkup periyatōr iṟumpūtiṉaip payakkumeṉka.
mēlum, ēḻuvakuppukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭa innāṭakak kataic cuvaiyāṉatu, tuvakkattiliruntu paṭippaṭiyē uyarntu naṭuniṟkum nāṉkām vakuppiṉkaṇ iṉi yuyartaṟku ellai illaiyāmaḷavil vantu niṟṟalum, ataṉ kaṭ collappaṭṭa turuvācar vacaimoḻiyāṉatu oru maṇik kōvaiyiṉai iṇaikkuṅ kokkuviṟpōla muṉpiṉ katai nikaḻcciyiṉait toṭarpupaṭuttuṅ karuviyāy uṟutalum niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟ pālaṉavākum. in nāṭakattiṉaip payilvār itaṉ naṭuniṟkum nāṉkām vakuppiṉai nōkkic celluṅkāṟ cakuntalai taṉ kaṇavaṉāl maṟantuviṭappaṭṭut taṉiyāḷāyt tuyarkūrntu niṟkum nilaiyiṉai niṉaintu kavalaiyum, uyarntōr nilaiyiṟṟappār eṉpataṉāl āṟutalum, iṉṉāmai vantakkāl iṟaivaṉaiyē tuṇaiyākap paṟṟutalun, turuvācar vacaimoḻiyiṉai yuṇarntu accamum, atu tīrtaṟkām maruntiṉai aṟiyappeṟutalāl maṉattērcciyuṅ, karuk koṇṭu kaṇavaṉāl maṟakkappaṭṭa cakuntalaiyiṉpāl irakkamum, avaḷ tantaiyār avaḷaiyum avaḷkūṭiya yāḻōr maṇattaiyum makiḻntēṟṟamai terintu uvakaiyuñ, cakuntalaiyaik kaṇavaṉ illattiṟku ēkuvittal vēṇṭi av vāciramattiluḷḷa makaḷir avaṭku maṅkaḷa vāḻttuc ceyyum muṟaikaṇṭu oruvakai makiḻcciyun tōḻimārum avaḷ tantaiyum avaḷaiviṭṭup piriyuṅkāṟ paṭum āṟṟāmaiyāl maṉa urukkamum, avaḷ tantaiyār avaṭkuk kūṟum aṟivuraiyiṉ vāymaiyāl ilalṟavuṇarcciyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyt tōṉṟiniṟṟaluṇarntu iṉpuṟumāṟu āciriyar itaṉai amaitta tiṟam peritu peritu
itaṉpiṉ, taṉpāṟpōnta cakuntalai taṉ kātaṉ maṉaiviyē eṉpataṉai niṉaivukūrātu avaḷai aracaṉ vilakkiyavuṭaṉ avaḷ ōr arampai mātiṉāl eṭukkappaṭṭu vāṉvaḻiyē maṟaintu pōyiṉāḷ eṉa moḻiyum avvaḷavil in nāṭakak katai muṭikkappaṭumāyiṟ, piṉṉum atu payilvārkku muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkaiyiṉait taṇiyāmai yiṟ kuṟaipāṭu uṭaittām. akkuṟaipāṭu nīkkutaṟku āṟām vakuppiliruntu āciriyar ikkataiyiṉai naṭāttuntiṟaṉ ciṟitu kāṭṭutum. cempaṭavaṉ oruvaṉ piṭitta mīṉ oṉṟaṉ akaṭṭiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa kaṇaiyāḻi oṉṟu tuṣiyanta maṉṉaṉ peyar cetukkappaṭṭatā yiruttal kaṇṭu kāvalāḷar ataṉai avvaracaṉpāṟ koṇarntu koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. aracaṉ taṉ peyar poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ataṉaik kāṇṭalun, turuvācar iṭṭa vacaimoḻiyiṉ tītu nīṅkap peṟṟāṉāyt, taṉṉāl vilakkappaṭṭa cakuntalaiyait tāṉ kātaṉmaṇam parintukoṇṭañāṉṟu tāṉ avaḷatu kaiviralil aṇinta kaṇaiyāḻiyē ata veṉṟum, avaḷ taṉpāl varukiṉṟuḻi vaḻiyilirunta oru vāvi nīril avaḷtāṉ aṟiyātapaṭiyē aḵtu avaḷ kaiyai viṭṭu naḻuvi viḻuntatākalvēṇṭameṉa avaḷuṭaṉ pōnta kautami ammaiyār kūṟiyatu vāymaiyē yākal vēṇṭumeṉṟun, tāṉ avaḷai maṇanta varalāṟukaḷai avaḷ taṉmuṉṉē eṭutturaikka avaṟṟait tāṉ poyyeṉak karutiyatellām niṉaiviḻappē yeṉṟum naṉku niṉaivukūrntu aracaṉ āṟṟoṇāt tuyarameytip pulampum vakaikaḷaiyollām āciriyar itaṉ āṟām vakuppiṉkaṇ melleṉa nuvaṉṟu mellac cellutal kāṇka.
iṉṉum, innāṭakak katai nikaḻcciyiṉaip paṭippaṭiyē uyarttuk koṇṭuceṉṟu, nāṉkām vakuppiṉ iṟutiyilē ōr āṟṟāmaikku iṭaṉāy ataṉai ucci yilēṟṟiṉa āciriyar, piṉṉar aintām vakuppilē mikkatoru tuyarattil atu kīḻ iṟaṅkumāṟu ceytu, ataṉ īṟṟil ataṉaik karaikāṇāta tuyarkkaṭalil amiḻttuvārāyk, katait talaiviyākiya cakuntalaiyaiyē kaṭpulaṉukkut teṉpaṭā vāṟu vāṉūṭu maṟaiyac ceyta tiṟaṉ ōrntukoḷka.
aṅṅaṉaṅ kataittalaiviyaiyun talaivaṉaiyun tuyark kaṭaluḷ amiḻtti, attuṇaiyil ataṉaiviṭṭup pōtal nallicaippulamai yākāmaiyiṉ, attuyark kaṭaluḷ amiḻntār aṉaivaraiyum ataṉiliruntum mēleṭuttu viṭutaṟku āṟām vakuppil āciriyaṉ ceyta cūḻccit tiṟaṉai iṉṉuñ ciṟitu kāṭṭutum, talaimakaḷ talaimakaṉ avark kuriyārai yellām peruntuyarkkaṭaluṭ paṭuppittaṟku ētuvāy niṉṟatu, aracaṉ cakuntalai kaiyiliṭṭa kaṇaiyāḻi uṟṟanērattil avaḷatu kaiviraliṉiṉṟuṅ kaḻaṉṟu oru peruntaṭattiṉuḷ amiḻntip pōṉa nikaḻcciyēyaṉṟō? aṅṅaṉam āḻntārai yellām ataṉilirunteṭuttu iṉpakkarai cērkkaluṟuvār, muṉṉē tāṉum āḻntu piṉṉē ellāraiyum iṭark kaṭaluḷ āḻttiya ak kaṇaiyāḻiyiṉai mutaṟkaṇ mēleṭuttalē ceyaṟpālatām. tāṉ āḻttiya poruḷai mēleṭutta viṭavē, ataṉāl āḻntā rellārum mēluyarntu karaicērntiṉpuṟṟiruttal tāṉākavē nikaḻum. ākavē, āciriyar kāḷitācar āṟām vakuppiṉ tuvakkattilēyē, akkaṇaiyāḻiyiṉai mēṟkoṇaralāyiṉāreṉka.
aṅṅaṉam ataṉai mēṟkoṇarumiṭattum, aintām vakuppiṉ iṟutiyil tuṉpattāl ūṭuruvappeṟṟu makiḻcciyai iḻantārkku, ataṉai mīṇṭum varuvittu avaruḷḷattai āṟṟutalaṉṟō ceyaṟpālatām īṇṭu āciriyaṉum aṅṅaṉañ ceytatuṇṭōveṉiṉ uṇṭu. tāṉ piṭitta mīṉiṉ vayiṟṟaik kīṇṭakkāl ataṉilirunteṭutta aracaṉatu kaṇaiyāḻiyai vilai ceyyak koṇarnta cempaṭavaṉai ūrkāvaṟkārarum avarkkut talaivaṉāṉa kottavālum piṭittuk koḷḷa, avaṉum avarum uraiyāṭum pakutikaḷ nakaiccuvai payattaluṭaṉ, avaṉaik koṭumaiyāka naṭattāmaṟ kottavāl avaṉ vāymoḻi yiṉ uṇmai kaṇṭu avaṉukku aracaṉiṭamiruntu paricu vāṅkik koṭuttu, avaṉuṭaṉ kēṇmai pārāṭṭic cellum pakutiyum mutaṟkaṭṭōṉṟiya nakaiccuvaiyiṉaic citaiyātāyp payilvāruṭaṉ ellāraiyum makiḻavaittal kāṇka. nāṉkām vakuppiliruntu uḷḷaṅkuḻaiyun tuṉpattiṉ pakkamāyt tirumpi, aintām vakuppiṉ īṟṟiṟ peruki nirampiya tuyarkkaṭaluṭ pōntu viḻunta innāṭakakkatai nikaḻcciyai āṟāmvakuppiṉ tuvakkattilēyē ataṉkaṇṇiruntum eṭuttu nakaiyum makiḻvum mēṉmēṉmikumāṟu ataṟku mīṇṭum uyirppēṟṟik koṇṭuceṉṟu, ēḻām vakuppiṉkaṇ āciriyaṉ ataṉai iṉitu muṭikkum ariya periya pulamaittiṟaṅ kaṇṭu iṉpuṟuka.
iṉi, innāṭakak katainikaḻccikku ōr accāṇi pōl iṭaippukuntu niṉṟa turuvācar vacaimoḻikku orumīṉai aṭaiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyuñ, cakuntalai tuṣiyantaṉai iṭai niṉṟu piṇikkuṅ kātal aṉpiṟku oru kaṇaiyāḻiyai aṭaiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyuṅ, kātalarāvār tammai oruṅku piṇippitta iṟaivaṉ tiruvaruṭṭuṇaiyiṉai nāṭātu tām oruvaraiviṭṭu oruvar pirinta nilaimaiyiṉaiyē niṉaintu tuyarkūrntu niṉṟāl avaratu uḷḷantiṭpam iṉṟi nīrpōl nekiḻnta patattatāyiruttaliṉ attakaiya vuḷḷattaiyē piṟariṭṭa vacaimoḻi paṟṟiyirukkum eṉpataṟku ammīṉ irukkum oru taṭākanīrai aṭaiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyun, taṉatu tuṇaiyai etirpārāta kātalarkkum avaratu aṉpiṉ mikutiyaip pārāṭṭi iṟaivaṉ ṟiruvaruḷē orukāṟ ṟuṇaiyāyp pōntuniṉṟu avaratuḷḷattaip paṟṟiya vacaimoḻiyiṉaip pōḻntu ataṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa avaratu kātalaṉpiṉai mīḷvittu miḷirac ceyyum eṉpataṟkut taṟceyalāyt tāṉ vīciya valaiyiṟ cikkuṇṭu pōnta oru mīṉiṉ vayiṟṟaip pōḻntu ataṉkaṇṇiruntum eṭutta aracaṉatu kaṇaiyāḻiyai veḷikkoṇarnta oru cempaṭavaṉatu ceyalai aṭaiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyum āciriyaṉ innāṭakattai ākkiyirukkum nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipula viyalpu uyttuṇarntu kaḷikkaṟ pālatā meṉka.
iṉik kaṇaiyāḻiyai kāṇāmuṉ aracaṉāl vilakkuṇṭu vāṉūṭu maṟaintu ēkiya cakuntalai evvāṟu āyiṉāḷ eṉavuṅ, kaṇaiyāḻiyaik kaṇṭapiṟ cakuntalaiyait tāṉ maṇanta varalāṟukaḷellām oru ciṟitum viṭāmal niṉaivu kūrntu āṟṟoṇāt tuyar koṇṭa aracaṉ evvā ṟāyiṉāṉ eṉavum avar tam muṭivu aṟiyum vēṭkai aṭaṅkappeṟātāy itaṉaip payilvōruḷḷan tuyaruṟumākaliṉ, ataṉaiyum viṭuvittaṟku āciriyaṉ itaṉ āṟām vakuppiṉ īṟṟilum, ēḻām vakuppiluñ ceyyuñ ceykait tiṟaṉaiyum īṇṭuk kāṭṭutum.
aracaṉ taṉatu kaṇaiyāḻiyaik kaṇṭatumutaṟ cakun talaiyaiyaṉṟip piṟitu etaṉaiyum niṉaiyamāṭṭāṉāyk kaḻiperuntuyar kūrntu, avaḷatu vaṭivaḻakai ōr ōviyamāka varaintavāṟē taṉatu iḷamarakkāvil iruppuḻip, piḷḷaip pēṟiṉṟi iṟantupōṉa peruñ celvarāṉa oru vaṇikariṉ ceyti kēṭṭut, taṉakkum putalvaṟpēṟiṉmaiyāl taṉatu nilaimaiyum aṅṅaṉantāṉ ākap pōkiṉṟateṉṉum eṇṇan tōṉṟa āṟṟakillā iṭumpaiyuṟṟu ayarntu meymmaṟantu kiṭakkiṉṟāṉ. annērattil, ō pārp pāṉaik kāppāṟṟu pārppāṉaik kāppāṟṟu eṉṉum ōlak kuraloli caṭutiyil eḻavē, aracaṉ uṭaṉē uṇarvukūṭi yeḻuntu, taṉ naṇpaṉum vitūṣakaṉumāṉa mātaviyaṉ eṉpāṉ kaṭ pulaṉākāta oru koṭumpēyāl mēleṭukkap paṭṭu araṇmaṉaiyiṉ uccittaḷattiṉkaṇ vaittuk kaḻuttu muṟikkappaṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉpa taṟintu, annoṭiyē villuṅ kaṇaiyum ēnti appēyait toṭarntu araṇmaṉaimēṟ cella, avaṉetirē intiraṉ tērppākaṉāṉa mātali varukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉaik kāṇṭalum aracaṉ ampai villiṉiṉṟum eṭuttuviṭṭu, avaṉ vantaceyti kēṭṭaṟintu, intiraṉukkup pakaivarāṉa ōr arakkar kūṭṭattait tolaikkumporuṭṭu avaṉatu vēṇṭukōṭpaṭiyē avaṉ koṇarnta vāṉavūrtiyil ēṟi intiraṉatu vāṉnakarkkuc celkiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum ittuṇaiyil ikkatai nikaḻcciyiṉai āciriyar āṟām vakuppiṉ kaṭṭoṭarpupaṭut tiruttaliṉ, itaṉaip payilvāratu uḷḷam aracaṉṟaṉ āṟṟā nilaimaiyiṉai yaṟintu, aḵtevvāṟu āyiṟṟeṉa mēlum aṟitaṟku avāviya vēṭkaiyiṉai iḵtu oru ciṟitu taṇivu ceytal kāṇka.
aṟṟāyiṉum, āṟām vakuppiṉkaṇ oru ciṟitu taṇivu ceyyappaṭṭa muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkai, vāṉulakiṟ ceṉṟa vēntaṉ evvāṟāyiṉāṉ eṉa aṟitaṟku mēluṅ kiḷarntu niṟkumākaliṉ, ataṉai muṟṟun taṇivuceytal yāṇṭeṉiṉ ataṟ kaṉṟē ēḻām vakuppu eḻunta teṉpatu ēḻām vakuppiṉ tuvakkattilēyē, tuṣiyanta maṉṉaṉ arakkarkaḷait tolaittu avvāṟṟāl tēvēntiraṟkup pērutavipurintu, avaṉ taṉakkuc ceyta peruñciṟappukkaḷai ēṟṟu, avaṉatu vāṉavūrtiyil ivarntu maṇṇulakukkut tirumpukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu nuvalappaṭṭamaiyāl, aracaṉatu vāṉ celavai aṟivāṉ vēṇṭi yeḻunta vēṭkaiyiṉai āciriyaṉ curuṅkiya kūṟṟilēyē taṇittāṉāyiṟṟu.
